| R12-COMMERCE-5091 | P2 | capability | **Product Scout finds price and availability differences** — Product Scout finds price/availability differences, auctions, liquidations, wholesale listings and supply shocks. |  | unit: The named crate test passes; mutating the function's core refusal or arithmetic fails it. | v11.6 p31 §20 Physical Commerce & Product Arbitrage |
| R12-COMMERCE-5092 | P2 | capability | **SKU resolver maps equivalents and detects variants and counterfeits** — The SKU/entity resolver maps equivalent products across retailers, marketplaces and regions and detects variants and counterfeits. |  | unit: The named crate test passes; mutating the function's core refusal or arithmetic fails it. | v11.6 p31 §20 Physical Commerce & Product Arbitrage |
| R12-COMMERCE-5093 | P2 | capability | **Commerce feasibility computes all-in economics** — Commerce feasibility computes all-in landed cost, expected resale, time-to-sale, return risk, inventory carry and capital lockup, refusing a candidate missing any input. |  | unit: The named crate test passes; mutating the function's core refusal or arithmetic fails it. | v11.6 p31 §20 Physical Commerce & Product Arbitrage |
| R12-COMMERCE-5094 | P0 | invariant | **Purchase executor enforces limits, account controls and fraud safeguards** — Purchases go only through merchant/marketplace adapters behind per-account and global limits, account/credential controls and fraud safeguards, refused before any merchant call. | `EXTERNAL_ACTION` `LIVE_CAPITAL` | unit: The named crate test passes; mutating the function's core refusal or arithmetic fails it. | v11.6 p31 §20 Physical Commerce & Product Arbitrage |
| R12-COMMERCE-5095 | P2 | capability | **Logistics brain plans route, carrier, warehouse, customs, insurance** — The logistics brain plans route, carrier, consolidation, warehouse, customs, insurance and delivery risk. |  | unit: The named crate test passes; mutating the function's core refusal or arithmetic fails it. | v11.6 p31 §20 Physical Commerce & Product Arbitrage |
| R12-COMMERCE-5096 | P1 | capability | **Inventory ledger holds units, condition, location, basis, reservations, ownership** — The physical inventory ledger records units, condition, location, cost basis, reservations and ownership, event-sourced and replayable. |  | unit: The named crate test passes; mutating the function's core refusal or arithmetic fails it. | v11.6 p31 §20 Physical Commerce & Product Arbitrage |
| R12-COMMERCE-5097 | P2 | capability | **Resale router chooses venue and prices and reprices** — The resale router chooses marketplace/auction/direct venue by net proceeds and prices and reprices listed inventory. |  | unit: The named crate test passes; mutating the function's core refusal or arithmetic fails it. | v11.6 p31 §20 Physical Commerce & Product Arbitrage |

## GOV

Governance, legal isolation, authority envelopes.

| ID | P | Kind | Requirement | Flags | Verification | Source |
|---|---|---|---|---|---|---|
| R12-GOV-9067 | P1 | constraint | **Planner agent cannot merge or deploy** — The planner agent role may read and plan but cannot merge or deploy. |  | inspection: The boundary is enforced by credentials/config or a gate, and a run attempting the forbidden action is refused. | GCP v2.1 p14 §13 Autonomous Development and AI Engineering |
| R12-GOV-9075 | P2 | constraint | **Learning agent proposals still pass promotion gates** — The learning agent may propose capability changes from incidents/reviews/benchmarks, but promotion stays gated. |  | inspection: The boundary is enforced by credentials/config or a gate, and a run attempting the forbidden action is refused. | GCP v2.1 p15 §13 Autonomous Development and AI Engineering |
| R12-GOV-9079 | P2 | process | **Architecture memory indexed for agents** — ADRs, contracts, service catalogue, SLOs, runbooks and the blueprint are indexed for agents. |  | acceptance: The index lists every ADR, blueprint source with hash, the deployable binaries, and runbooks; a missing entry fails. | GCP v2.1 p15 §13 Autonomous Development and AI Engineering |
| R12-GOV-9080 | P2 | process | **Agents cite the rule they change when proposing an exception** — An agent proposing an exception must cite the rule it is changing. |  | inspection: A PR template field naming the rule is required and checked. | GCP v2.1 p15 §13 Autonomous Development and AI Engineering |
| R12-GOV-10004 | P0 | invariant | **Organization Policy enforces non-bypassable guardrails** — Organization Policy constraints enforce platform guardrails (e.g. no service-account keys, no external IPs, resource locations) so no project-level actor can bypass them. |  | plan: Policies are declared and an acceptance test asserts each; deleting the policy resource fails the test. | GCP v2.1 p19 §19 Complete GCP Service Catalog for This Platform |
| R12-GOV-10005 | P2 | capability | **Cloud Asset Inventory records asset and change history** — Cloud Asset Inventory (feeds or exports) records every resource and its changes so drift and unmanaged resources can be found. | `MANAGED_SERVICE` | plan: An asset feed/export resource exists and its sink is readable; removing it fails a plan assertion. | GCP v2.1 p19 §19 Complete GCP Service Catalog for This Platform |
| R12-GOV-10109 | P0 | invariant | **Validated results reach the hot path only as signed packages** — A validated research result becomes a strategy/model/risk/capital proposal and reaches execution only through promotion gates as signed policy/model packages, never as an unreviewed direct hot-path call. |  | integration: Cells apply only verified signed payloads; nothing in the edge node depends on cognitive crates. | GCP v2.1 p25 §21.2 World event to cognitive response |
| R12-GOV-10149 | P0 | constraint | **prod: live bounded execution with strict isolation and signed artifacts** — prod runs live bounded execution with strict region/project isolation, reserved capacity and signed artifacts only. | `LIVE_CAPITAL` | plan: Prod admits only attested digests in an isolated project with reservations. | GCP v2.1 p27 §24 Environment and Promotion Topology |
| R12-GOV-1017 | P0 | invariant | **Autonomous operation only inside bounded authority** — Autonomous discovery, research, training, promotion, deployment, allocation and operation happen only inside bounded authority. |  | unit: Autonomy changes go only through AutonomyController::request_change with an authenticated operator; a live ceiling stops the process. | v11.6 p3 §1.1 Product Identity |
| R12-GOV-1042 | P0 | invariant | **AI may originate but act only inside a pre-authorized envelope** — AI systems may originate hypotheses, models, strategies, forecasts, explanations, opportunity intents and capital recommendations and act automatically only inside a pre-authorized operating envelope. |  | unit: An edge cell refuses to trade with no verified capital envelope or an expired one. | v11.6 p4 §1.2 Non-Negotiable Safety/Correctness Boundary |
| R12-GOV-3029 | P0 | invariant | **Communications & Conduct Brain gates external communications** — Every authorized external communication passes truthfulness/provenance, identity/disclosure, channel policy, audience/jurisdiction and market-conduct checks before release. | `EXTERNAL_ACTION` | unit: A draft failing any single check is blocked; removing one check lets a failing draft through and its independence test fails. | v11.6 p17 §9.1 Specialist Brain Society |
| R12-GOV-3030 | P2 | capability | **Post-publication monitoring of external communications** — The Conduct Brain monitors communications after publication. | `EXTERNAL_ACTION` | integration: A published message is re-checked after publication and a later violation raises a finding. | v11.6 p17 §9.1 Specialist Brain Society |
| R12-GOV-3031 | P0 | invariant | **Block deceptive or manipulative influence patterns** — Deceptive or manipulative influence methods have no policy and any plan or communication using one is refused. |  | unit: A plan using a deceptive method is infeasible; removing the is_deceptive check fails the test. | v11.6 p17 §9.1 Specialist Brain Society |
| R12-GOV-4077 | P0 | invariant | **Regulated functions only through permitted legal entities** — Deposit-taking, third-party lending, brokerage, derivatives dealing and similar regulated functions run only through appropriate legal entities, counterparties and jurisdictional permissions. | `EXTERNAL_ACTION` | integration: A regulated function is refused unless an (entity, counterparty, jurisdiction) permission is recorded; the empty registry refuses everything. | v11.6 p26 §13 Capital Brain & Capital Bank |
| R12-GOV-6116 | P0 | invariant | **Eligibility metadata on venue, product, user, jurisdiction, strategy** — Technical capability and legal permission are separate dimensions: every venue, product, user/entity, jurisdiction and strategy carries eligibility metadata. |  | unit: Each of the five subject kinds has an eligibility record and an absent record refuses. | v11.6 p39 §25 Governance, Legal Isolation & Authority |
| R12-GOV-6117 | P1 | invariant | **Cognition may analyse what execution may not do** — The cognitive core may evaluate an opportunity or intervention that the execution layer, acting identity, entity, channel or jurisdiction is not permitted to perform. |  | integration: An out-of-scope opportunity routes to a shadow record with no path to an intent; an unauthorised intervention is analysed and reported refused. | v11.6 p39 §25 Governance, Legal Isolation & Authority |
| R12-GOV-6118 | P0 | constraint | **Jurisdiction/product eligibility gate before deployment and new venue** — A jurisdiction/product eligibility gate runs before strategy deployment and before every new venue or instrument becomes executable. |  | integration: Deploying a strategy or enabling a venue/instrument without an eligible record is refused. | v11.6 p39 §25 Governance, Legal Isolation & Authority |
| R12-GOV-6119 | P0 | constraint | **Separate identities and credentials per regulated function** — Trading, custody/transfer, market creation, banking/financing and commerce purchasing each use separate identities and credentials. | `LIVE_CAPITAL` `EXTERNAL_ACTION` | inspection: Each function's credential is a distinct secret readable only by the process using it. | v11.6 p39 §25 Governance, Legal Isolation & Authority |
| R12-GOV-6120 | P0 | constraint | **Signed authority envelopes for destinations, leverage, products, functions** — Human-signed or governance-signed authority envelopes govern destinations, leverage, product classes and regulated functions. |  | unit: An envelope without a valid signature over every bound is refused; widening requires a new signed envelope. | v11.6 p39 §25 Governance, Legal Isolation & Authority |
| R12-GOV-6121 | P0 | invariant | **Immutable audit of model, data, policy, code and control versions** — Each decision has an immutable audit of the model, data, policy, code and control versions involved. |  | integration: A decision record carries all five version identifiers and sits in the hash-chained log; dropping one fails a replay test. | v11.6 p39 §25 Governance, Legal Isolation & Authority |
| R12-GOV-6122 | P0 | invariant | **Unsupported opportunities remain shadow-only** — Research/simulation may be broader than executable scope, and unsupported opportunities remain shadow-only however they are rescored. |  | unit: An opportunity outside the scope stays shadow however it is rescored. | v11.6 p39 §25 Governance, Legal Isolation & Authority |
| R12-GOV-6123 | P0 | contract | **Action tool registration declares six attributes and revocation** — Every action tool has a registered acting identity, permission scope, disclosure policy, audience/channel rules, rate/budget limits and revocation path. |  | unit: A registration missing any field is refused; after revocation the next call is refused and audited. | v11.6 p39 §25 Governance, Legal Isolation & Authority |
| R12-GOV-6124 | P0 | contract | **Goal approval, plan approval and execution separately auditable** — Goal approval, plan approval and action execution are three separately auditable records. |  | unit: One intervention leaves three linked records each with its own actor and time; out-of-order timestamps are refused. | v11.6 p39 §25 Governance, Legal Isolation & Authority |
| R12-GOV-6125 | P0 | constraint | **Sensitive intervention classes require stronger approval** — Sensitive intervention classes can require stronger approval than ordinary research. |  | unit: A capital-class intent with only operational authority is refused; with capital authority admitted. | v11.6 p39 §25 Governance, Legal Isolation & Authority |
| R12-GOV-6126 | P0 | constraint | **Market-facing actions cross-checked for manipulation risk** — Public communications and market-facing actions are cross-checked for market-conduct/manipulation risk. |  | unit: A market-facing action matching a manipulation pattern is refused before any adapter. | v11.6 p39 §25 Governance, Legal Isolation & Authority |
| R12-GOV-6127 | P0 | constraint | **Profit or price impact alone never justifies an action** — Public communications and market-facing actions cannot be justified solely by expected profit or desired price impact. |  | unit: A justification of only profit and/or price impact is refused; one with an evidence ground is admitted. | v11.6 p39 §25 Governance, Legal Isolation & Authority |

## SEC

Security and trust.

| ID | P | Kind | Requirement | Flags | Verification | Source |
|---|---|---|---|---|---|---|
| R12-SEC-8062 | P0 | constraint | **Admin access only via IAP/workforce identity/PAM** — Administrative access must use IAP, workforce identity and PAM: no public SSH/RDP, and privileged access must be temporary and audited. |  | acceptance: No machine has an external IP; SSH ingress only from the IAP range; privileged roles granted via PAM entitlements. | GCP v2.1 p5 §5 Network and Connectivity Architecture |
| R12-SEC-8100 | P0 | capability | **Short-lived workload certificates via CAS; KMS/HSM roots; IAM-mapped identities** — Certificate Authority Service must issue short-lived workload certificates, Cloud KMS/HSM must protect signing roots/policy keys, and broker identities must map to GCP service accounts/IAM. | `MANAGED_SERVICE` `NEW_DEPENDENCY` | integration: A broker refuses a client without a valid CAS-issued cert; policy keys are KMS-held. | GCP v2.1 p9 §7.3 Fabric placement on GCP |
| R12-SEC-8118 | P0 | invariant | **Workload Identity + Secret Manager; no long-lived credentials stored** — Workloads must authenticate via Workload Identity and read secrets via Secret Manager/CSI mounts; long-lived cloud credentials must not be persisted (e.g. in Kubernetes Secrets). | `KUBERNETES` | acceptance: Every Cloud Run service mounts secrets as files; SA key creation/upload is refused by org policy. | GCP v2.1 p9 §8 Regional GKE Service Plane |
| R12-SEC-9060 | P1 | constraint | **Workforce/admin paths use Cloud Identity/IAM/IAP/PAM** — Internal workforce and admin paths use Cloud Identity, IAM, IAP and Privileged Access Manager. | `MANAGED_SERVICE` | plan: Every admin surface sits behind IAP and privileged roles are granted only via PAM entitlements. | GCP v2.1 p14 §12 Edge, Portal, API and Causal Action Plane |
| R12-SEC-9064 | P1 | constraint | **Crawler/scout isolated in dedicated project/VPC/pools and egress identity** — Crawler/scout workloads run in a dedicated project/VPC/node pools with their own egress identity. | `KUBERNETES` `MANAGED_SERVICE` | plan: Scouts have their own identity and network and only they hold information-source egress. | GCP v2.1 p14 §12 Edge, Portal, API and Causal Action Plane |
| R12-SEC-9065 | P0 | invariant | **Untrusted web content cannot invoke high-authority tools or production secrets** — Untrusted web content cannot directly invoke high-authority tools or reach production secrets. |  | acceptance: Ingested content has no code path to tool invocation or secret reads. | GCP v2.1 p14 §12 Edge, Portal, API and Causal Action Plane |
| R12-SEC-9068 | P0 | constraint | **Coder agent has sandboxed credentials, no prod secrets** — The coder agent works with sandboxed credentials and never holds production secrets. |  | inspection: The boundary is enforced by credentials/config or a gate, and a run attempting the forbidden action is refused. | GCP v2.1 p14 §13 Autonomous Development and AI Engineering |
| R12-SEC-9070 | P1 | constraint | **Security findings severity controls promotion** — Security-agent findings are triaged by a severity policy that controls promotion. |  | inspection: The boundary is enforced by credentials/config or a gate, and a run attempting the forbidden action is refused. | GCP v2.1 p14 §13 Autonomous Development and AI Engineering |
| R12-SEC-9077 | P1 | constraint | **Agent sandboxes: ephemeral, egress-limited, quota, read-only telemetry, synthetic data** — Agent sandboxes are ephemeral namespaces/projects with egress policy, quota, read-only production telemetry by default and synthetic/test data when possible. | `KUBERNETES` `MANAGED_SERVICE` | plan: A sandbox identity has only read on telemetry and no data-store grants. | GCP v2.1 p15 §13 Autonomous Development and AI Engineering |
| R12-SEC-9078 | P1 | constraint | **One policy-enforcing, audited model gateway for agents** — Agents use approved models only through one policy-enforcing gateway; credentials stay in Secret Manager and requests are audited. | `MANAGED_SERVICE` `EXTERNAL_ACTION` | integration: Every agent model request passes the gateway, which records it; a direct call fails. | GCP v2.1 p15 §13 Autonomous Development and AI Engineering |
| R12-SEC-9084 | P0 | invariant | **Agent sandboxes have no production credentials** — Ephemeral agent sandboxes (Cloud Run Jobs, GKE Jobs or build workers) never have production credentials available. | `KUBERNETES` | plan: No production-scoped grant exists for any sandbox identity. | GCP v2.1 p15 §13.1 Autonomous Development at Superintelligence Scale |
| R12-SEC-9096 | P0 | invariant | **CI uses WIF short-lived credentials; no stored service-account keys** — GitHub Actions exchanges OIDC for short-lived GCP credentials through Workload Identity Federation, and no service-account key is stored anywhere. |  | acceptance: No google_service_account_key in Terraform, org policy disables key creation/upload, and workflows authenticate via the WIF provider. | GCP v2.1 p16 §14.2 Source-to-production flow |
| R12-SEC-9109 | P0 | invariant | **KMS/HSM signs release, model and policy manifests** — Cloud KMS/HSM signs release, model and policy manifests. | `MANAGED_SERVICE` | integration: Each manifest signature verifies with a KMS public key; a manifest signed with any other key is refused. | GCP v2.1 p16 §14.2 Source-to-production flow |
| R12-SEC-9110 | P0 | invariant | **Binary Authorization requires trusted attestations at deploy** — Binary Authorization and admission policy require trusted attestations for deployed workloads. | `KUBERNETES` | acceptance: Default rule is REQUIRE_ATTESTATION with ENFORCED_BLOCK_AND_AUDIT_LOG naming the attestor. | GCP v2.1 p16 §14.2 Source-to-production flow |
| R12-SEC-9122 | P0 | constraint | **Least-privilege, group-based IAM** — Human and workload identity uses Cloud Identity/IAM with least privilege and group-based access. |  | plan: The control exists in Terraform and is asserted by a test or plan. | GCP v2.1 p17 §15 Security and Trust Architecture |
| R12-SEC-9123 | P1 | constraint | **Privileged Access Manager time-bounded elevation** — Sensitive operations use Privileged Access Manager for time-bounded elevation with approvals. | `MANAGED_SERVICE` | plan: The control exists in Terraform and is asserted by a test or plan. | GCP v2.1 p17 §15 Security and Trust Architecture |
| R12-SEC-9124 | P0 | constraint | **External CI uses Workload Identity Federation** — GitHub/external CI obtains short-lived GCP access through Workload Identity Federation without service-account keys. |  | plan: The control exists in Terraform and is asserted by a test or plan. | GCP v2.1 p17 §15 Security and Trust Architecture |
| R12-SEC-9125 | P1 | constraint | **Workload Identity for GKE pods; no node-wide credentials** — GKE pods map to GCP service identities with no node-wide credentials. | `KUBERNETES` | plan: The control exists in Terraform and is asserted by a test or plan. | GCP v2.1 p17 §15 Security and Trust Architecture |
| R12-SEC-9126 | P0 | constraint | **Secret Manager with rotation, versioning, audit** — Venue/API credentials and non-key secrets live in Secret Manager with rotation, versioning and audit, delivered as files. |  | plan: The control exists in Terraform and is asserted by a test or plan. | GCP v2.1 p17 §15 Security and Trust Architecture |
| R12-SEC-9127 | P0 | constraint | **KMS/HSM for CMEK, signing, attestation roots** — Cloud KMS/HSM provides CMEK, package signing, high-assurance key material and attestation roots. |  | plan: The control exists in Terraform and is asserted by a test or plan. | GCP v2.1 p17 §15 Security and Trust Architecture |

| R12-SEC-9130 | P0 | constraint | **Cloud Armor WAF/DDoS/rate controls internet-facing** — Every internet-facing surface has Cloud Armor WAF, DDoS (adaptive protection) and rate controls. |  | plan: The control exists in Terraform and is asserted by a test or plan. | GCP v2.1 p17 §15 Security and Trust Architecture |
| R12-SEC-9131 | P1 | constraint | **Hierarchical firewall / NGFW egress segmentation** — Organisation/folder hierarchical firewall policies (Cloud NGFW) provide network policy, egress segmentation and narrow lateral paths. |  | plan: The control exists in Terraform and is asserted by a test or plan. | GCP v2.1 p17 §15 Security and Trust Architecture |

| R12-SEC-9135 | P1 | constraint | **Cloud Audit Logs and Asset Inventory** — Cloud Audit Logs and Cloud Asset Inventory provide administrative/data-access evidence and change visibility. |  | plan: The control exists in Terraform and is asserted by a test or plan. | GCP v2.1 p17 §15 Security and Trust Architecture |
| R12-SEC-9136 | P0 | constraint | **IAP for internal/admin web; no public management endpoints** — Internal/admin web access uses IAP and no management endpoint is public. |  | plan: The control exists in Terraform and is asserted by a test or plan. | GCP v2.1 p17 §15 Security and Trust Architecture |
| R12-SEC-9137 | P0 | invariant | **Custody, deploy-signing, Fabric identity and user-auth keys are separate trust domains** — Custody/transfer keys, deployment-signing keys, Fabric identity keys and user-auth keys are separate trust domains. |  | plan: Each key class is a distinct KMS key with disjoint IAM. | GCP v2.1 p17 §15 Key separation |
| R12-SEC-9138 | P0 | invariant | **Engineering-agent compromise cannot yield capital-moving authority** — Compromise of an engineering agent must not yield authority to move capital. |  | inspection: No CI/agent identity holds any treasury/withdrawal grant. | GCP v2.1 p17 §15 Key separation |
| R12-SEC-9139 | P0 | invariant | **Trading-service compromise cannot yield deployment-signing authority** — Compromise of a trading service must not yield deployment-signing authority. |  | plan: No workload identity holds signer rights on the attestor key. | GCP v2.1 p17 §15 Key separation |
| R12-SEC-10002 | P1 | constraint | **Workforce identity comes from Cloud Identity** — Human (workforce) access to the platform's GCP resources and admin surfaces authenticates through Cloud Identity accounts, not ad-hoc or shared identities. |  | inspection: Every human principal granted in Terraform/IAP is a managed user/group; a literal shared account or allUsers grant fails. | GCP v2.1 p19 §19 Complete GCP Service Catalog for This Platform |
| R12-SEC-10003 | P0 | constraint | **Privileged access is just-in-time through PAM** — Privileged (admin/break-glass) access to production projects is granted just-in-time and time-bounded through Privileged Access Manager rather than standing role bindings. | `MANAGED_SERVICE` | plan: A PAM entitlement exists for each privileged role and no human holds that role as a standing binding; a standing owner/editor binding fails. | GCP v2.1 p19 §19 Complete GCP Service Catalog for This Platform; GCP v2.1 p27 §26 Recommended Build Order |
| R12-SEC-10006 | P1 | capability | **Security Command Center reports posture with no hot-path dependency** — Security Command Center collects posture findings for every project; nothing in the trading path depends on it. | `MANAGED_SERVICE` | plan: SCC custom modules are planned in each environment; the edge-node and brains link no SCC client. | GCP v2.1 p19 §19 Complete GCP Service Catalog for This Platform; GCP v2.1 p23 §19.0B Security, Delivery & Operations |
| R12-SEC-10007 | P0 | constraint | **Cloud Audit Logs capture admin and data-access evidence** — Admin-activity and data-access audit logs are enabled for every service holding secrets, keys, ledger, evidence or model data. |  | plan: An IAM audit config enables DATA_READ/DATA_WRITE for each sensitive service; removing a service from it fails the test. | GCP v2.1 p20 §19 Complete GCP Service Catalog for This Platform |
| R12-SEC-10013 | P1 | constraint | **Agent and crawler web egress is policy-controlled and audited** — Agent and crawler web egress passes a policy/audit proxy (Secure Web Proxy where suitable) so every outbound destination is allowed by policy and logged. |  | inspection: Egress from agent/scout workloads is only possible through the proxy and the proxy allowlist is declared; a direct route fails. | GCP v2.1 p20 §19 Complete GCP Service Catalog for This Platform; GCP v2.1 p22 §19.0B Foundation & Network |
| R12-SEC-10019 | P1 | constraint | **Cloud Armor WAF/DDoS/rate policy on the public edge** — Every internet-facing backend has a Cloud Armor policy with WAF, adaptive DDoS protection and per-client rate limiting; the edge may deny clients without trading impact. |  | inspection: Every security policy enables layer-7 adaptive protection and a rate-based ban; removing either fails the tests. | GCP v2.1 p20 §19 Complete GCP Service Catalog for This Platform; GCP v2.1 p21 §19.0B Foundation & Network |
| R12-SEC-10021 | P2 | constraint | **Certificate Manager issues public TLS certificates** — Public TLS certificates for the edge are issued and rotated by Certificate Manager (or managed certificates), never uploaded by hand. | `MANAGED_SERVICE` | inspection: Certificate resources are managed; a self-managed certificate block fails. | GCP v2.1 p20 §19 Complete GCP Service Catalog for This Platform |
| R12-SEC-10022 | P0 | invariant | **IAP protects every admin interface** — Admin interfaces (consoles, Argo CD, Kargo, node shells) are reachable only through Identity-Aware Proxy. |  | inspection: Remote-admin firewall rules admit only the IAP range and every admin service has an IAP binding; a rule from 0.0.0.0/0 fails. | GCP v2.1 p20 §19 Complete GCP Service Catalog for This Platform |
| R12-SEC-10023 | P1 | constraint | **Hierarchical firewall policies segment networks** — Network segmentation is enforced by Cloud NGFW / hierarchical firewall policies inherited from the organisation, in addition to per-VPC rules. |  | plan: A hierarchical/network firewall policy is planned and associated; absence fails. | GCP v2.1 p20 §19 Complete GCP Service Catalog for This Platform |
| R12-SEC-10029 | P0 | invariant | **Critical VMs boot as Shielded VMs** — Every VM-based critical service boots with Secure Boot, vTPM and integrity monitoring. |  | inspection: Every instance template enables the three shielded options; removing one fails. | GCP v2.1 p20 §19 Complete GCP Service Catalog for This Platform |
| R12-SEC-10031 | P1 | constraint | **Cloud Service Mesh provides mTLS for warm services only** — Warm services communicate over mTLS with traffic policy (Cloud Service Mesh), and the mesh never carries Reflex traffic. | `KUBERNETES` `MANAGED_SERVICE` | plan: Warm-service traffic is mTLS; reflex nodes are outside the mesh. | GCP v2.1 p20 §19 Complete GCP Service Catalog for This Platform |
| R12-SEC-10047 | P0 | constraint | **Images are vulnerability-scanned before release** — Every image is vulnerability-scanned (Artifact Analysis metadata) and a failing scan blocks release. |  | inspection: deploy.yml scans before push/sign and fails on CRITICAL; removing the step fails the pipeline gate test. | GCP v2.1 p21 §19 Complete GCP Service Catalog for This Platform |
| R12-SEC-10049 | P0 | invariant | **CI and workloads authenticate keylessly via WIF** — GitHub Actions and external CI obtain short-lived credentials through Workload Identity Federation; no service-account key exists anywhere. |  | inspection: The pipeline authenticates via the WIF provider, no key resource exists, and org policy disables key creation; adding a key fails. | GCP v2.1 p21 §19 Complete GCP Service Catalog for This Platform; GCP v2.1 p24 §20 Critical Service-to-Service Connection Matrix; GCP v2.1 p22 §19.0B Security, Delivery & Operations |
| R12-SEC-10050 | P0 | invariant | **IAM is deny-by-default and least privilege per workload** — Every workload has its own identity with only the grants it uses; nothing runs as the default compute identity. |  | inspection: Each deployable has its own SA, none uses the default compute SA, and every SA runs or signs something. | GCP v2.1 p19 §19 Complete GCP Service Catalog for This Platform; GCP v2.1 p22 §19.0B Security, Delivery & Operations |
| R12-SEC-10051 | P0 | invariant | **Secrets come from Secret Manager, never Git or environment** — Venue, API and service secrets are held in Secret Manager, read by authorized workloads only, mounted as files with bounded startup behaviour; no secret is in Git. |  | inspection: No secret value in Terraform; every secret is mounted as a file and granted per workload; venue credential unreadable where live trading is impossible. | GCP v2.1 p21 §19 Complete GCP Service Catalog for This Platform; GCP v2.1 p22 §19.0B Security, Delivery & Operations |
| R12-SEC-10052 | P0 | constraint | **KMS provides CMEK and signing; runtime survives key outage** — Cloud KMS provides CMEK and release/package/ledger signing keys; key availability is required for new promotions but not for the currently running package. |  | inspection: Keys rotate and data keys cannot be destroyed; the running edge node holds no KMS client on its decision path. | GCP v2.1 p21 §19 Complete GCP Service Catalog for This Platform; GCP v2.1 p22 §19.0B Security, Delivery & Operations; GCP v2.1 p23 §19.0B Security, Delivery & Operations |
| R12-SEC-10053 | P1 | constraint | **Cloud HSM for higher-assurance keys where required** — Signing and key material requiring higher assurance uses Cloud HSM protection. | `COST` | plan: kms_protection_level=HSM in environments that require it. | GCP v2.1 p21 §19 Complete GCP Service Catalog for This Platform |
| R12-SEC-10054 | P0 | constraint | **Private CA issues mTLS identity for Fabric and VMs** — Certificate Authority Service issues private mTLS identities for Fabric brokers, clients and VMs. | `MANAGED_SERVICE` `NEW_DEPENDENCY` | plan: A CA pool exists and Fabric clients/brokers present certificates from it. | GCP v2.1 p21 §19 Complete GCP Service Catalog for This Platform; GCP v2.1 p27 §26 Recommended Build Order |
| R12-SEC-10055 | P0 | constraint | **VPC Service Controls perimeter around data, AI and ledger** — A VPC Service Controls perimeter encloses data, AI and ledger services to prevent exfiltration. | `MANAGED_SERVICE` | plan: A service perimeter covering the data/AI/ledger APIs is planned. | GCP v2.1 p21 §19 Complete GCP Service Catalog for This Platform |
| R12-SEC-10056 | P2 | capability | **Sensitive Data Protection discovers and redacts sensitive data** — Sensitive Data Protection discovers, classifies and redacts sensitive data in stores and logs. | `MANAGED_SERVICE` | plan: Discovery config/inspect templates exist for data stores. | GCP v2.1 p21 §19 Complete GCP Service Catalog for This Platform |
| R12-SEC-10157 | P0 | process | **Agent compromise simulation proves boundaries hold** — A drill gives an autonomous dev agent malicious instruction/input and verifies secret, network, merge and prod-deploy boundaries prevent authority escalation. |  | game-day: Drill record shows each boundary refused. | GCP v2.1 p27 §25 Resilience Testing and Game Days |
| R12-SEC-10183 | P0 | invariant | **Autonomous agents cannot gain capital authority, bypass policy or hold long-lived keys** — Autonomous agents cannot obtain production capital/custody authority, cannot merge/deploy around policy, and cannot access long-lived cloud keys. |  | integration: Autonomy changes require an authenticated operator identity; no SA keys exist; prod deploy refused from workflows. | GCP v2.1 p28 §27 Architecture Acceptance Criteria; GCP v2.1 p30 §31 Final Platform Definition |
| R12-SEC-10201 | P0 | invariant | **AI agents must not change production by kubectl, SSH or console** — An AI agent must not change production via kubectl/SSH or console clicks; only GitOps or a pre-approved audited runbook. |  | inspection: No agent identity holds production write/SSH/kubectl grants; shells only via IAP. | GCP v2.1 p29 §28 Explicit Anti-Patterns; GCP v2.1 p30 §31 Final Platform Definition |
| R12-SEC-7014 | P0 | constraint | **Keys in Cloud KMS/HSM with segregated custody/signing** — Secrets and keys are held in Cloud KMS/HSM plus segregated custody/signing systems. | `MANAGED_SERVICE` | plan: Plan renders KMS keys; signing keys are not readable by workloads that do not sign. | v11.6 p40 §26 Platform & Deployment Architecture |
| R12-SEC-7015 | P0 | invariant | **No browser access to keys, ledger internals or execution nodes** — The browser has no access to keys, ledger internals or execution nodes. |  | acceptance: Acceptance scan finds no frontend route or API surface exposing key material, ledger internals or node endpoints. | v11.6 p40 §26 Platform & Deployment Architecture |

## CICD

CI/CD, supply chain, GitOps, autonomous development.

| ID | P | Kind | Requirement | Flags | Verification | Source |
|---|---|---|---|---|---|---|
| R12-CICD-8008 | P1 | capability | **Engineering plane: Cloud Build pools, Artifact Registry, GitOps controllers** — The engineering/control plane must provide an immutable supply chain and GitOps promotion via GitHub, Cloud Build private pools, Artifact Registry, Config Sync and Argo CD/Kargo/Rollouts. | `KUBERNETES` `MANAGED_SERVICE` | inspection: Images are built in a private pool, stored in Artifact Registry by digest and promoted only by Kargo/Argo commits; a manual deploy must be refused by policy. | GCP v2.1 p2 §1 Executive Architecture |
| R12-CICD-8030 | P0 | invariant | **Deployments by signed immutable digests only** — Git and signed image digests must be deployment truth: no mutable tags are deployed. |  | acceptance: Binary Authorization requires an attestation and workflows deploy by digest; a tag-only reference must be refused. | GCP v2.1 p3 §2 Non-Negotiable Architecture Rules |
| R12-CICD-8031 | P0 | invariant | **No secret values in Git** — No secret value may be committed to Git. |  | acceptance: check-secrets.sh over the tree and history reports nothing; committing a key fixture must fail CI. | GCP v2.1 p3 §2 Non-Negotiable Architecture Rules |
| R12-CICD-8032 | P1 | constraint | **No console-only changes or hand-copied models** — Production state must not be changed through console-only actions, and models must not be hand-copied into production. |  | inspection: Drift detection (plan) shows no unmanaged change; model activation requires a registry-signed artifact. | GCP v2.1 p3 §2 Non-Negotiable Architecture Rules |
| R12-CICD-8033 | P0 | process | **Autonomous agents obey the same production pipeline** — Autonomous agents may create PRs, run experiments and diagnose incidents, but any production change they cause must pass tests, attestations, promotion policy and rollback like any other change. |  | inspection: Agent identities hold no deploy permission outside the pipeline; a direct deploy by the agent identity is refused. | GCP v2.1 p3 §2 Non-Negotiable Architecture Rules |
| R12-CICD-8117 | P1 | process | **GitOps split: Config Sync, Argo CD, Kargo, Argo Rollouts** — Config Sync must own cluster baseline/policy/CRDs, Argo CD applications, Kargo environment promotion, and Argo Rollouts progressive rollout. | `KUBERNETES` `MANAGED_SERVICE` | inspection: Each controller is installed and owns only its layer. | GCP v2.1 p9 §8 Regional GKE Service Plane |
| R12-CICD-9066 | P0 | invariant | **Autonomous development never bypasses Git, test, attestation or deploy policy** — Agent-produced code and infrastructure changes go through Git, tests, attestation and deployment policy like any other change. |  | inspection: Deploy requires a passing CI run on a repository commit and signs only that; a fork/PR commit cannot be signed. | GCP v2.1 p14 §13 Autonomous Development and AI Engineering |
| R12-CICD-9069 | P0 | constraint | **Test agent cannot waive failing risk or performance gates** — The test/replay agent cannot waive a failing risk or performance gate. |  | inspection: The boundary is enforced by credentials/config or a gate, and a run attempting the forbidden action is refused. | GCP v2.1 p14 §13 Autonomous Development and AI Engineering |
| R12-CICD-9072 | P1 | constraint | **Reviewer independent of coder by model and context** — The reviewer agent uses a separate model/context from the primary coder. |  | inspection: The boundary is enforced by credentials/config or a gate, and a run attempting the forbidden action is refused. | GCP v2.1 p15 §13 Autonomous Development and AI Engineering |
| R12-CICD-9073 | P0 | constraint | **Release agent promotes by immutable digest; no direct kubectl/SSH** — The release agent updates environment desired state by immutable digest after gates and has no direct kubectl/SSH to production. |  | inspection: The boundary is enforced by credentials/config or a gate, and a run attempting the forbidden action is refused. | GCP v2.1 p15 §13 Autonomous Development and AI Engineering |
| R12-CICD-9076 | P2 | constraint | **Agent workers are ephemeral with scoped service accounts** — Agent orchestration runs on a management plane and execution workers are ephemeral jobs/build workers with scoped service accounts. | `KUBERNETES` `MANAGED_SERVICE` | plan: Each agent task runs in an ephemeral worker under a task-scoped identity. | GCP v2.1 p15 §13 Autonomous Development and AI Engineering |
| R12-CICD-9081 | P0 | invariant | **No invisible changes: repairs become commits/PRs or audited runtime actions** — Any autonomous repair that changes code/config becomes a normal Git commit/PR, or a pre-approved runtime action whose event is durably audited. |  | integration: A remediation produces an audit record before the effect; an effect without a record fails. | GCP v2.1 p15 §13 Autonomous Development and AI Engineering |

| R12-CICD-9083 | P2 | process | **Orchestrator keeps dependency DAGs and prevents overlapping edits** — An agent orchestrator maintains dependency DAGs and prevents multiple agents editing the same ownership boundary without merge coordination. |  | inspection: Two tasks claiming the same boundary are serialised. | GCP v2.1 p15 §13.1 Autonomous Development at Superintelligence Scale |

| R12-GCP-9015 | P3 | constraint | **Persistent cognitive services on regional GKE; burst on Vertex/accelerators** — Persistent cognitive services run primarily on regional/multi-region GKE Standard; burst training, evaluation and simulation use Vertex AI, GKE accelerator pools and batch compute. | `KUBERNETES` `MANAGED_SERVICE` `COST` | plan: A plan shows the cognitive services on the stated runtime. | GCP v2.1 p11 §11.1 Superintelligence Service Topology |
| R12-GCP-9086 | P0 | constraint | **Terraform/GitOps is the only normal path to persistent cloud state** — Agents may propose services/models/tools, but Terraform/GitOps remains the only normal path to persistent cloud state. |  | inspection: infra apply consumes a saved plan; drift detectors raise findings. | GCP v2.1 p15 §13.1 Autonomous Development at Superintelligence Scale |
| R12-GCP-9120 | P1 | process | **Terraform: gated apply, plan artifact, change windows for core resources** — Terraform changes go through plan plus gated apply; folder/project/network/database changes have explicit change windows and a plan artifact. |  | inspection: Apply consumes the saved plan artifact; core changes are refused outside a window. | GCP v2.1 p16 §14.3 Component-specific release mechanics |
| R12-GCP-9183 | P2 | capability | **GKE services autoscale via HPA/VPA, lag metrics, Cluster Autoscaler** — GKE services scale with HPA/VPA on custom lag/queue metrics and Cluster Autoscaler for warm/batch workloads. | `KUBERNETES` | plan: HPA objects exist. | GCP v2.1 p19 §18 Scaling and Performance Model |
| R12-GCP-10001 | P1 | constraint | **Projects and folders isolate blast radius per environment** — The resource hierarchy (folders/projects) isolates each environment and plane so a failure or policy change in one project cannot reach another; the hierarchy is never a runtime dependency. |  | inspection: Each committed tfvars names a distinct project and nothing at runtime calls Resource Manager; reusing one project id across two environments fails the check. | GCP v2.1 p19 §19 Complete GCP Service Catalog for This Platform; GCP v2.1 p21 §19.0B Foundation & Network |
| R12-GCP-10008 | P1 | constraint | **Isolated Reflex, Fabric, Service, Data and Engineering VPCs** — Separate VPC networks exist for Reflex, Fabric, Service, Data and Engineering traffic, each deny-by-default on ingress. |  | plan: Five google_compute_network resources with deny-ingress rules are planned; merging two into one fails the module tests. | GCP v2.1 p20 §19 Complete GCP Service Catalog for This Platform |
| R12-GCP-10009 | P2 | capability | **Network Connectivity Center hub-spoke with route filters** — VPCs and hybrid links connect through an NCC hub/spoke with route filters, and regional trading continues when remote spokes are unavailable. | `MANAGED_SERVICE` `MULTI_REGION` | plan: An NCC hub and spokes with include/exclude ranges are planned; a spoke without filters fails. | GCP v2.1 p20 §19 Complete GCP Service Catalog for This Platform; GCP v2.1 p21 §19.0B Foundation & Network; GCP v2.1 p29 §29 Current GCP Capability Validation Notes (2026) |
| R12-GCP-10010 | P2 | capability | **Cloud Router provides dynamic BGP for Interconnect and VPN** — Hybrid links (Interconnect, HA VPN) exchange routes dynamically through Cloud Router BGP sessions. | `MANAGED_SERVICE` | plan: A router with BGP peers for each attachment/tunnel is planned. | GCP v2.1 p20 §19 Complete GCP Service Catalog for This Platform |
| R12-GCP-10011 | P2 | capability | **Private provider connectivity with HA VPN fallback** — Provider, colo, custody and IBM connectivity uses Dedicated/Partner/Cross-Cloud Interconnect where available, with HA VPN or internet fallback only where acceptable. | `MANAGED_SERVICE` `EXTERNAL_ACTION` | plan: Interconnect attachment and HA VPN gateway are planned together; an attachment without fallback fails the module test. | GCP v2.1 p20 §19 Complete GCP Service Catalog for This Platform; GCP v2.1 p21 §19.0B Foundation & Network |
| R12-GCP-10012 | P1 | constraint | **Cloud NAT controls egress for non-latency-critical workloads** — Internet egress for scouts, agents and builders goes through Cloud NAT with logging; Reflex venue egress is governed separately from that path. |  | inspection: Every NAT translates only named subnets to reserved addresses and logs; a NAT covering all subnets fails. | GCP v2.1 p20 §19 Complete GCP Service Catalog for This Platform; GCP v2.1 p22 §19.0B Foundation & Network |
| R12-GCP-10014 | P2 | constraint | **Private Service Connect for managed APIs, never on the hot path** — Managed Google/service endpoints are reached via Private Service Connect where supported, and no PSC hop sits on the trading hot path. | `MANAGED_SERVICE` | plan: A PSC endpoint is planned for Google APIs; none fronts the execution node venue path. | GCP v2.1 p20 §19 Complete GCP Service Catalog for This Platform; GCP v2.1 p21 §19.0B Foundation & Network; GCP v2.1 p22 §19.0B Foundation & Network |
| R12-GCP-10015 | P1 | constraint | **Private Google Access on every subnet** — Every subnet reaches Google APIs over Private Google Access without public addresses. |  | inspection: Every google_compute_subnetwork sets private_ip_google_access=true; one without fails the test. | GCP v2.1 p20 §19 Complete GCP Service Catalog for This Platform |
| R12-GCP-10030 | P1 | constraint | **Regional GKE Standard hosts warm services, solvers, agents and sinks** — Warm domain/control services, solvers, agents and sinks run on regional GKE Standard clusters with a regional control plane and multi-zone node pools. | `KUBERNETES` `MANAGED_SERVICE` | plan: Regional GKE Standard clusters with multi-zone pools are planned per region. | GCP v2.1 p20 §19 Complete GCP Service Catalog for This Platform; GCP v2.1 p22 §19.0B Execution & Messaging; GCP v2.1 p29 §29 Current GCP Capability Validation Notes (2026) |
| R12-GCP-10032 | P1 | constraint | **Cloud Run only where cold-start and latency fit the SLO** — Cloud Run/Cloud Run Jobs host bursty stateless APIs, scouts, webhooks and agent jobs, and are not used where cold-start or latency violates an SLO. |  | inspection: Every Cloud Run service on a latency-sensitive path holds a warm minimum instance; an SLO-bound service at min 0 fails. | GCP v2.1 p20 §19 Complete GCP Service Catalog for This Platform; GCP v2.1 p22 §19.0B Execution & Messaging |
| R12-GCP-10060 | P2 | capability | **Connectivity Tests verify network reachability** — Network Intelligence Center Connectivity Tests verify intended and forbidden paths. | `MANAGED_SERVICE` | plan: Connectivity test resources for key paths exist. | GCP v2.1 p21 §19 Complete GCP Service Catalog for This Platform |
| R12-GCP-10143 | P1 | constraint | **Six environments: dev, integration, replay, paper, staging, prod** — The platform provides dev, integration, replay, paper, staging and prod environments, each with its stated purpose and data/connectivity posture. |  | inspection: Each environment has committed tfvars and a project. | GCP v2.1 p27 §24 Environment and Promotion Topology |
| R12-GCP-10144 | P0 | invariant | **dev uses mock venues and no production secrets** — dev uses synthetic/mock venues, reduced data and no production secrets. |  | inspection: dev declares the simulated venue adapter and shares no project with any other environment. | GCP v2.1 p27 §24 Environment and Promotion Topology |
| R12-GCP-10165 | P1 | process | **Build phase 6: Regional service plane** — Phase 6 (Regional service plane) delivers: GKE asset/capital/risk/evidence/API services, Fabric consumer scaling, Redis cache, meeting its exit test before later phases depend on it. | `KUBERNETES` | inspection: Each listed deliverable exists and its exit test passes. | GCP v2.1 p28 §26 Recommended Build Order |
| R12-GCP-10199 | P1 | invariant | **Must not use one global GKE cluster or one flat VPC** — The platform must not run a single global GKE cluster or a single giant flat VPC. | `KUBERNETES` | inspection: Multiple VPCs per environment; any cluster is regional and not shared across regions. | GCP v2.1 p29 §28 Explicit Anti-Patterns |
| R12-GCP-7001 | P1 | constraint | **Place workloads by lane: Google Cloud plus IBM Quantum** — Workloads are placed by time lane (reflex, warm, slow/training, ledger, fabric, quantum) rather than one runtime everywhere; application/data/compute run on Google Cloud and QPU workloads on IBM Quantum. | `MANAGED_SERVICE` | inspection: Terraform and the image matrix place reflex, warm and quantum workloads on distinct runtimes; a module that put the edge node on Cloud Run would fail. | v11.6 p39 §26 Platform & Deployment Architecture |
| R12-GCP-7002 | P1 | constraint | **Reflex cells run on dedicated C3/C3D-class low-latency VMs** — Regional Reflex Cells run as Rust binaries on dedicated GCE C3/C3D-class (or equivalent) low-latency VMs. | `MULTI_REGION` `COST` | plan: terraform plan for an environment with one execution node renders a c3-class instance running qip-edge-node; a non-C3 machine_type is refused. | v11.6 p39 §26 Platform & Deployment Architecture |
| R12-GCP-7005 | P1 | constraint | **Stateless burst warm services run on Cloud Run** — Warm services that are stateless and burst-shaped run on Cloud Run where serverless economics are beneficial. | `MANAGED_SERVICE` | plan: terraform plan renders google_cloud_run_v2_service for the catalogue workloads. | v11.6 p39 §26 Platform & Deployment Architecture |
| R12-GCP-7006 | P2 | constraint | **Stateful long-running warm services run on GKE** — Stateful/long-running platform services run on GKE. | `KUBERNETES` `MANAGED_SERVICE` | plan: terraform plan renders a GKE cluster hosting the stateful services. | v11.6 p39 §26 Platform & Deployment Architecture |
