Public edge, portal/BFF, identity, API boundary.

| ID | P | Kind | Requirement | Flags | Verification | Source |
|---|---|---|---|---|---|---|
| R12-API-8054 | P1 | constraint | **Public entry via DNS, global LB, Cloud Armor, CDN, Certificate Manager** — User traffic to portal/API must enter through Cloud DNS → Global External Application LB → Cloud Armor → Cloud CDN/Certificate Manager → Cloud Run (or GKE Gateway), with no direct access to internal planes. | `MANAGED_SERVICE` | plan: Plan with public_edge set shows the LB chain with security policy and CDN; a Cloud Run service with public ingress must be refused. | GCP v2.1 p5 §5 Network and Connectivity Architecture |
| R12-API-8113 | P1 | constraint | **Internal APIs behind internal LB with mTLS/service policy** — Regional internal APIs must be entered through an internal Application LB/GKE Gateway with mTLS and service policy. | `KUBERNETES` `MANAGED_SERVICE` | plan: Plan shows internal LB; calls without client identity are refused. | GCP v2.1 p9 §8 Regional GKE Service Plane |
| R12-API-9057 | P1 | constraint | **Public edge: Cloud DNS, global ALB, Cloud Armor, CDN, Certificate Manager** — The public edge is Cloud DNS plus a global external Application Load Balancer with Cloud Armor, Cloud CDN and Certificate Manager. | `MANAGED_SERVICE` | plan: A plan for an environment with a public edge creates the LB, security policy, CDN backend and certificate map. | GCP v2.1 p14 §12 Edge, Portal, API and Causal Action Plane |
| R12-API-9058 | P2 | constraint | **Static content cached; dynamic BFF on Cloud Run behind serverless backends** — Static content is cached at the edge and the dynamic BFF runs on Cloud Run behind serverless backends. | `MANAGED_SERVICE` | inspection: Every backend bucket is CDN-served and the BFF is a serverless NEG backend. | GCP v2.1 p14 §12 Edge, Portal, API and Causal Action Plane |
| R12-API-9059 | P1 | constraint | **Customer identity on Identity Platform with strong MFA/passkeys** — Customer/application identities use Identity Platform with strong MFA/passkeys where supported. | `MANAGED_SERVICE` | plan: Identity Platform config has MFA mandatory (or enforced per tenant) and passkeys enabled. | GCP v2.1 p14 §12 Edge, Portal, API and Causal Action Plane |
| R12-API-9061 | P0 | invariant | **Public API never exposes ledger tables, broker ports, Reflex nodes or keys** — The public API exposes portfolio/ledger-derived views and authorised commands only, never direct ledger tables, Fabric broker ports, Reflex nodes or custody keys. |  | acceptance: The route table contains no route resolving to a ledger table, broker, node or key, and every mutating route raises a typed intent; adding such a route fails the suite. | GCP v2.1 p14 §12 Edge, Portal, API and Causal Action Plane |
| R12-API-10017 | P1 | constraint | **Global ALB is the only public ingress for portal and API** — Public client traffic reaches portal/BFF/API only via HTTPS Global External ALB fronting Cloud Run (or GKE Gateway). |  | plan: The ALB/forwarding rules exist and every Cloud Run service ingress is internal or internal-and-cloud-load-balancing; a public run.app ingress fails. | GCP v2.1 p20 §19 Complete GCP Service Catalog for This Platform; GCP v2.1 p21 §19.0B Foundation & Network; GCP v2.1 p24 §20 Critical Service-to-Service Connection Matrix |
| R12-API-10020 | P2 | constraint | **Cloud CDN accelerates public static content only** — Public static assets are served through Cloud CDN with an explicit cache mode; dynamic/API backends are not cached. |  | inspection: Every backend bucket enables CDN with explicit cache mode and no backend service does. | GCP v2.1 p20 §19 Complete GCP Service Catalog for This Platform |

## RES

Failure, degradation, disaster recovery, game days.

| ID | P | Kind | Requirement | Flags | Verification | Source |
|---|---|---|---|---|---|---|
| R12-RES-8025 | P1 | invariant | **Loss of global cognition or another region cannot stop a healthy cell** — A healthy Reflex Cell must keep operating when global cognition or any other region is lost. |  | integration: A cell with its mesh link severed keeps placing paper orders within its envelope; making the pass require the centre must fail. | GCP v2.1 p3 §2 Non-Negotiable Architecture Rules |
| R12-RES-8026 | P1 | contract | **Every dependency has a degradation envelope** — Each dependency of a Reflex Cell must have a declared degradation envelope stating how the cell behaves as that input ages or disappears. |  | unit: Each capability in the degradation table has a freshness bound and multiplier; a capability with no row must be refused at configuration. | GCP v2.1 p3 §2 Non-Negotiable Architecture Rules |
| R12-RES-8038 | P1 | capability | **Zonal failure tolerated by standby, cross-zone replicas, multi-zone plane** — A single-zone loss must be survived through a Reflex standby in another zone, Fabric RF3 partition replicas across zones, and a regional multi-zone service plane. | `KUBERNETES` | game-day: Killing one zone leaves the cell, fabric quorum and warm services serving. | GCP v2.1 p4 §3 Global Region and Failure-Domain Strategy |
| R12-RES-8039 | P1 | capability | **Regional failure: healthy regions continue, critical topics mirrored** — On regional failure healthy regions must continue, critical Fabric topics must have selective cross-region mirrors, and ledger/data services stay available per their managed topology. | `MULTI_REGION` | game-day: Disabling a region leaves other cells trading and mirrored P0/P1 topics readable elsewhere. | GCP v2.1 p4 §3 Global Region and Failure-Domain Strategy |
| R12-RES-8103 | P1 | process | **Rolling broker upgrade never loses quorum** — Broker upgrades must proceed one broker at a time — drain leadership, verify replica health, replace image, rejoin — never upgrading enough replicas to lose quorum. |  | game-day: An upgrade run keeps P0/P1 produce available throughout. | GCP v2.1 p9 §7.3 Fabric placement on GCP |
| R12-RES-9087 | P2 | process | **SRE agents turn regressions into replay/fuzz/game-day tests first** — SRE agents learn from incidents and turn each regression into a replay/fuzz/game-day test before proposing a fix. |  | inspection: Each incident record links a new test that fails before the fix. | GCP v2.1 p15 §13.1 Autonomous Development at Superintelligence Scale |
| R12-RES-9166 | P0 | invariant | **Reflex standby stays fenced; failure loses only that shard's venue session** — On a Reflex process/VM failure the standby stays fenced until failover criteria are met and only that shard's venue connection is lost. | `MULTI_REGION` | game-day: Killing the primary does not activate the standby until criteria pass; other shards keep sessions. | GCP v2.1 p18 §17 Availability, Resilience and Disaster Recovery |
| R12-RES-9167 | P0 | process | **Reflex recovery after liveness, fencing, venue-session checks; no zero-gap promise** — Reflex recovers by restart or standby promotion only after liveness, fencing and venue-session checks; recovery targets are measured per venue and zero-gap failover is not promised. |  | game-day: A drill records per-venue recovery time and the promotion follows the three checks. | GCP v2.1 p18 §17 Availability, Resilience and Disaster Recovery |
| R12-RES-9168 | P0 | invariant | **Zone loss: Fabric RF3 retains quorum; Reflex promotes standby** — On loss of one zone, Fabric RF3 and the regional control plane retain quorum and Reflex can promote its standby. | `MULTI_REGION` | game-day: Zone-loss drill keeps quorum-acked records and promotes the standby. | GCP v2.1 p18 §17 Availability, Resilience and Disaster Recovery |
| R12-RES-9169 | P0 | invariant | **Broker failure: leader moves, producers refresh, quorum-acked records preserved** — On a Fabric broker failure the partition leader moves to a follower, producers refresh the map, and quorum-acked records are preserved before the broker is replaced and rebalanced. |  | game-day: Killing a broker loses no acked record. | GCP v2.1 p18 §17 Availability, Resilience and Disaster Recovery |
| R12-RES-9170 | P0 | invariant | **Fabric region loss: Reflex continues on cached policy; mirrored topics elsewhere** — On loss of a whole Fabric region, Reflex continues under cached policy, critical mirrored topics remain available elsewhere, and the region is rebuilt from replicas/GCS archives. | `MULTI_REGION` | game-day: With the broker unreachable the cell passes on its last verified policy; spool pressure halts only per the journal wire. | GCP v2.1 p18 §17 Availability, Resilience and Disaster Recovery |
| R12-RES-9171 | P1 | process | **Regional control-plane loss: Reflex continues; rebuild from Terraform and GitOps** — On regional GKE (control plane) loss, Reflex and Fabric journaling continue, other regions take research/control work, and the cluster is rebuilt from Terraform + Config Sync + Argo. | `KUBERNETES` `MULTI_REGION` | game-day: Deleting the control plane leaves cells passing; terraform + argo rebuilds it. | GCP v2.1 p18 §17 Availability, Resilience and Disaster Recovery |
| R12-RES-9172 | P0 | invariant | **Global cognition loss: no new models; trading follows degradation envelope** — On loss of global cognition/AI, no new models or research are produced and local trading follows its freshness/degradation envelope with no hot-path impact. |  | integration: With every centre-fed capability stale, the cell's sizing multiplier falls per the table and passes continue. | GCP v2.1 p18 §17 Availability, Resilience and Disaster Recovery |
| R12-RES-9173 | P1 | metric | **Spanner leader move: monitor outcome-to-ledger backlog** — When the Spanner leader region moves, the ledger tolerates latency change and the outcome-to-ledger backlog is monitored. | `MANAGED_SERVICE` | unit: The backlog gauge is recorded and alerted. | GCP v2.1 p18 §17 Availability, Resilience and Disaster Recovery |
| R12-RES-9174 | P2 | constraint | **Bigtable failover via multi-cluster routing and app profiles** — Bigtable uses multi-cluster routed reads for failover, app profiles define behaviour, and single-cluster transactional workloads fail over explicitly with replay from Fabric/GCS. | `MANAGED_SERVICE` `MULTI_REGION` | plan: Instance has ≥2 clusters and app profiles. | GCP v2.1 p18 §17 Availability, Resilience and Disaster Recovery |
| R12-RES-9175 | P1 | constraint | **GCS research data per residency; derived stores rehydrated from immutable objects** — GCS uses replicated/dual-region locations per residency policy, regional landing is the source segment until archived, and derived stores can be rehydrated from immutable objects. | `MULTI_REGION` | integration: A derived store rebuilt from archived segments equals the original. | GCP v2.1 p18 §17 Availability, Resilience and Disaster Recovery |
| R12-RES-9176 | P1 | invariant | **GitOps plane loss: workloads keep running; recreate from Git** — On GitOps management-plane loss, running workloads continue, reconciliation pauses, and the plane is recreated from Git. | `KUBERNETES` | game-day: Stopping Argo leaves services serving. | GCP v2.1 p18 §17 Availability, Resilience and Disaster Recovery |
| R12-RES-9177 | P1 | constraint | **Registry region loss: nodes keep local images; secondary repository before prod** — On Artifact Registry region loss existing nodes keep local images and rollout pauses; release artifacts are replicated to a designated secondary repository before prod promotion. | `MULTI_REGION` | plan: A secondary repository exists and promotion to prod checks the artifact is there. | GCP v2.1 p18 §17 Availability, Resilience and Disaster Recovery |
| R12-RES-9178 | P1 | invariant | **Autonomous dev plane is never an availability dependency** — If the autonomous development plane is lost, no autonomous changes happen, production keeps running, and the human/manual pipeline remains available. |  | inspection: Every deploy/infra workflow is human-dispatchable and no runtime component calls an agent service. | GCP v2.1 p19 §17 Availability, Resilience and Disaster Recovery |
| R12-RES-10119 | P1 | process | **SLO/replay gates trigger rollback on regression** — SLO and replay gates observe production; a regression triggers rollback and feeds evidence back to the agent loop. |  | integration: A degraded release fails the gate and the previous digest is restored. | GCP v2.1 p25 §21.4 Autonomous development change |
| R12-RES-10124 | P0 | invariant | **Fabric loss: reflex continues, spools boundedly, narrows on pressure** — If the Fabric is lost, Reflex continues under local policy, buffers bounded critical events to a local spool, and narrows when spool or freshness policy is exceeded. |  | integration: Spool pressure reads Narrow then Exhausted and the wired cell halts new exposure; ship never blocks. | GCP v2.1 p26 §22 Dependency Degradation Contracts |
| R12-RES-10125 | P0 | process | **Fabric loss: platform alerts immediately and replays segments** — If the Fabric is lost the platform alerts immediately, restores the cluster and replays local spools and GCS segments. |  | game-day: An alert fires on broker loss; replay from spool/archive restores continuity. | GCP v2.1 p26 §22 Dependency Degradation Contracts |
| R12-RES-10126 | P0 | invariant | **Warm services loss: reflex continues locally without new authority** — If regional warm services are lost, Reflex continues local execution with no new warm policy/capital intelligence, Fabric keeps journaling, and other regions may cover eligible functions. |  | integration: A cut-off cell keeps placing paper orders inside its envelope, shrinks as inputs age and stops at envelope expiry. | GCP v2.1 p26 §22 Dependency Degradation Contracts |
| R12-RES-10127 | P0 | invariant | **Ledger loss: trade only within cached envelope until ledger-lag threshold** — If Spanner is lost, Reflex continues only within its cached capital/risk envelope until a ledger-lag threshold, with stricter transfer/position actions disabled. |  | integration: Orders stop when the envelope expires or the ledger-lag threshold is crossed; transfers are refused while lagging. | GCP v2.1 p26 §22 Dependency Degradation Contracts |
| R12-RES-10128 | P0 | invariant | **Ledger loss: outcomes accumulate durably and replay idempotently** — If Spanner is lost, outcome partitions accumulate durably in the Fabric and the ledger writer replays idempotently when restored. |  | property: Writer down leaves the checkpoint; on recovery balances equal a clean delivery. | GCP v2.1 p26 §22 Dependency Degradation Contracts |
| R12-RES-10129 | P1 | invariant | **Analytics store loss has no hot-path impact** — Loss of Bigtable/BigQuery has no direct hot-path impact; research/replay/features degrade while Fabric/GCS preserve source history. |  | inspection: The edge node's dependency closure contains no Bigtable/BigQuery client. | GCP v2.1 p26 §22 Dependency Degradation Contracts |
| R12-RES-10130 | P1 | invariant | **AI platform loss: no new cognition, existing packages continue** — If the AI/agent platform is lost, no new cognitive/model output is produced, existing packages continue, and research backlog queues. |  | integration: Node trades identically with the centre silent. | GCP v2.1 p26 §22 Dependency Degradation Contracts |
| R12-RES-10131 | P0 | invariant | **Cross-region loss: local trading continues, peer-dependent cycles disabled** — If cross-region networking is lost, local trading continues, multi-region cycles requiring peers are disabled, Fabric mirrors lag and catch up when the link returns, and regional durability stays intact. | `MULTI_REGION` | integration: A dark region suspends mirrored legs into it and leaves home-only trading unchanged; an unreadable wire treats all others as dark. | GCP v2.1 p26 §22 Dependency Degradation Contracts; GCP v2.1 p28 §27 Architecture Acceptance Criteria |
| R12-RES-10132 | P0 | invariant | **Feed loss stops dependent strategies and follows a local playbook** — If a venue feed is lost, strategies depending on the stale feed stop, positions are hedged or exited per a local playbook, and incident and reconciliation events are journaled. |  | integration: A stalled venue feed gets no new order past max_staleness while other venues keep trading; a playbook action is journaled. | GCP v2.1 p26 §22 Dependency Degradation Contracts |
| R12-RES-10133 | P1 | invariant | **GitOps loss leaves running system unchanged; break-glass reconciles later** — If GitHub/GitOps is lost the running system is unchanged and no new deploys occur; emergency changes use a pre-approved break-glass process later reconciled to Git. |  | inspection: Cloud Run services carry deletion-policy abandon; a break-glass runbook path exists. | GCP v2.1 p26 §22 Dependency Degradation Contracts |
| R12-RES-10148 | P1 | constraint | **staging validates scale, failure and game days with separate credentials** — Staging provides production-like scale and failure/game-day validation with separate accounts/credentials, canary packages and no uncontrolled live capital. |  | plan: Stage project has its own identities and canary promotion. | GCP v2.1 p27 §24 Environment and Promotion Topology |
| R12-RES-10151 | P1 | process | **Quarterly regional-loss game day** — A quarterly game day removes one execution region from service/control networking and verifies other regions continue, mirrors/recovery behave, and no cross-region arbitrage assumes the missing peer. | `MULTI_REGION` | game-day: A scheduled run record with observed outcomes exists each quarter. | GCP v2.1 p27 §25 Resilience Testing and Game Days |
| R12-RES-10152 | P1 | process | **Monthly zonal failure game day** — A monthly game day terminates the Reflex primary or Fabric zone replicas and proves fencing, quorum, failover and bounded outcome loss. |  | game-day: Monthly drill record with measured outcome loss. | GCP v2.1 p27 §25 Resilience Testing and Game Days |
| R12-RES-10153 | P1 | process | **Broker backpressure drill: priority QoS and bounded memory** — A drill freezes a sink and fills consumer lag, verifying priority QoS and no broker memory explosion. |  | game-day: Broker memory stays bounded and P0/P1 keep priority under a frozen sink. | GCP v2.1 p27 §25 Resilience Testing and Game Days |
| R12-RES-10154 | P0 | process | **Ledger outage drill: P1 retention, capital freshness, idempotent catch-up** — A drill denies ledger writes and verifies Fabric P1 retention, local capital freshness degradation, then idempotent catch-up. |  | game-day: Drill record shows no lost or doubled outcomes. | GCP v2.1 p27 §25 Resilience Testing and Game Days |
| R12-RES-10155 | P1 | process | **Bad model drill: degraded candidate blocked or rolled back** — A drill promotes an intentionally degraded candidate in nonprod/staging and verifies the shadow/replay/SLO gate blocks or rolls it back. |  | game-day: Degraded candidate refused or rolled back. | GCP v2.1 p27 §25 Resilience Testing and Game Days |
| R12-RES-10158 | P1 | process | **Periodic rebuild from zero proves no console dependency** — Periodically rebuild a regional cluster and a nonprod Fabric cluster from Terraform/Git/artifacts, proving no undocumented console dependency. |  | game-day: A scheduled up/down run from empty succeeds. | GCP v2.1 p27 §25 Resilience Testing and Game Days |
| R12-RES-10181 | P0 | invariant | **Region loss does not halt healthy regions** — Region loss does not globally halt healthy regions; cross-region cycles that depend on failed peers are disabled explicitly. | `MULTI_REGION` | integration: Darkening one region leaves other cells' home trading unchanged and suspends only mirrored legs into it. | GCP v2.1 p28 §27 Architecture Acceptance Criteria |
| R12-RES-10186 | P1 | process | **Every critical component is exercised in scheduled game days with RTO/RPO** — Every critical component is exercised in scheduled game days and has observable RTO/RPO or a documented reason exact guarantees cannot be promised. |  | game-day: A schedule and per-component RTO/RPO measurements exist. | GCP v2.1 p29 §27 Architecture Acceptance Criteria |
| R12-RES-7093 | P0 | invariant | **Degrade by capability, never fail as one monolith** — Each capability loss narrows what the platform does rather than halting the whole system. |  | unit: Losing one capability changes only the sizing multiplier; absence reads as unavailable; multipliers compound. | v11.6 p44 §28 Failure and Degradation Model |
| R12-RES-7094 | P0 | invariant | **Global cognition unavailable: last valid packs, size narrows, no new policy** — When global cognition is unavailable regional nodes continue with last valid packs until expiry, size narrows as confidence/TTL decays, and no new research/policy is issued. |  | integration: Cut-off cell keeps trading at reduced size and stops after payload expiry. | v11.6 p45 §28 Failure and Degradation Model |
| R12-RES-7095 | P1 | invariant | **Peer mesh degraded: local trading continues, coordination reduced** — When the peer reflex mesh is degraded local trading continues and distributed cycles needing coordination are disabled or reduced to pre-positioned directional bands. |  | integration: With peers unreachable, single-venue strategies trade and multi-cell cycles are refused. | v11.6 p45 §28 Failure and Degradation Model |
| R12-RES-7096 | P0 | invariant | **Quantum unavailable: classical path runs without execution interruption** — When quantum is unavailable the classical solver/model path runs and execution is not interrupted. |  | acceptance: Reflex compile set excludes qip-quantum; classical baseline always computed. | v11.6 p45 §28 Failure and Degradation Model |
| R12-RES-7097 | P0 | invariant | **Poisoned/stale source: quarantine evidence, downweight dependents** — A poisoned or stale data source has its evidence quarantined and dependent beliefs/models downweighted or invalidated. |  | integration: Quarantining a source downweights/invalidates beliefs citing it. | v11.6 p45 §28 Failure and Degradation Model |
| R12-RES-7098 | P0 | invariant | **Ledger degraded: halt new money-risking actions; cancels continue** — If authoritative accounting cannot be committed, new money-risking actions halt while safe cancels/flattening continue under local journal rules. |  | integration: Exhausted journal halts new exposure while withdrawals and fill confirmations continue. | v11.6 p45 §28 Failure and Degradation Model |
| R12-RES-7099 | P0 | invariant | **Custody/treasury unavailable: trade only settled capital** — When custody/treasury is unavailable trading is constrained to available settled capital with no new transfer dependence. | `LIVE_CAPITAL` | integration: With custody down, sizing uses settled capital only. | v11.6 p45 §28 Failure and Degradation Model |
| R12-RES-7100 | P0 | invariant | **Venue failure: quarantine, cancel, reroute/hedge, reconcile async** — On venue failure the venue is quarantined, exposure cancelled if possible, flow rerouted/hedged elsewhere and reconciled asynchronously. |  | integration: A rejecting venue is quarantined until cooldown and orders reroute to a healthy venue. | v11.6 p45 §28 Failure and Degradation Model |
| R12-RES-7101 | P0 | invariant | **Model drift: fall back to baseline/deterministic, shrink capital** — On model drift the system falls back to a challenger/baseline model or deterministic strategy and shrinks capital automatically. |  | integration: Drift detection triggers fallback and reduced sizing. | v11.6 p45 §28 Failure and Degradation Model |
| R12-RES-7102 | P0 | invariant | **Tick sequence gap/corrupt book: mark unreliable, snapshot, quarantine** — On a tick-feed sequence gap or corrupt book the affected interval/instrument is marked unreliable, a snapshot/recovery requested, derived features quarantined and contaminated windows kept out of training/replay until integrity is restored. |  | integration: A gap marks exactly the affected books unreliable and raises a snapshot request until it closes. | v11.6 p45 §28 Failure and Degradation Model |
| R12-RES-7103 | P1 | invariant | **Clock sync degraded: widen uncertainty, disable precise-ordering work** — When clock sync degrades raw timestamps are preserved, temporal uncertainty widened, strategies/research needing precise cross-venue ordering disabled and precision-sensitive labels/replay blocked from promotion. |  | integration: A degraded clock widens MarketEvent uncertainty and disables ordering-sensitive strategies. | v11.6 p45 §28 Failure and Degradation Model |
| R12-RES-7104 | P1 | invariant | **Replay diverges from live: reduce capital, recalibrate, re-promote** — When replay diverges from live execution, confidence/capital on dependent strategies is reduced, the twin recalibrated with live telemetry, benchmarks rerun and renewed promotion required. |  | integration: Divergence above threshold reduces dependent strategy size and blocks restoration until re-promotion. | v11.6 p45 §28 Failure and Degradation Model |
| R12-RES-7105 | P1 | invariant | **World models diverge: preserve branches, no false consensus** — Diverging world models are not forced into consensus: branches preserved, uncertainty raised, authority reduced where appropriate, discriminating evidence requested, arbitration by calibration and outcome history. |  | unit: Arbitration keeps every view, can branch/abstain/request information. | v11.6 p45 §28 Failure and Degradation Model |
| R12-RES-7106 | P0 | invariant | **Symbolic contradiction: infeasible, keep trace, fail closed on money** — On symbolic contradiction/unsatisfied constraints the conclusion/action is marked infeasible or unresolved, the trace retained, alternates searched, and money-moving invariants fail closed. |  | unit: A contradictory derivation yields infeasible with trace and blocks money movement. | v11.6 p45 §28 Failure and Degradation Model |
| R12-RES-7107 | P1 | invariant | **Ambient storm: rate-limit attention, dedupe, penalise, quarantine** — An ambient storm is handled by rate-limiting/budgeting attention, clustering duplicates, penalising low-value producers, quarantining drifting ambient models and protecting reflex/ledger resources. |  | unit: A storm never exceeds the attention budget; duplicates cluster; noisy producer reputation falls. | v11.6 p45 §28 Failure and Degradation Model |
| R12-RES-7108 | P0 | invariant | **Causal effect not identifiable: no causal claim, no autonomy scaling** — When a causal effect is not identifiable the system does not claim causation, downgrades confidence, keeps competing explanations, seeks evidence or safer experiments, and never scales autonomy from correlation alone. |  | unit: widen() refuses an unidentified attribution; weakly identified plans are never executed. | v11.6 p45 §28 Failure and Degradation Model |
| R12-RES-7109 | P0 | invariant | **Compromised action identity: revoke, stop, preserve audit, re-authorise** — On action/channel identity compromise the adapter and credentials are revoked/quarantined, pending actions stopped, audit preserved, identities/secrets rotated and explicit re-authorisation required; no unapproved identity is substituted. |  | unit: After revoke the next action is refused and audited. | v11.6 p46 §28 Failure and Degradation Model |
| R12-RES-7110 | P0 | invariant | **Conduct gate failure blocks the action; profit never overrides** — If the communication/conduct gate fails the external action is blocked, the failed plan and reasons retained, and a truthful alternative sought or the action abstained; profitability never overrides conduct. | `EXTERNAL_ACTION` | unit: A failing check blocks; a profit-only justification is refused. | v11.6 p46 §28 Failure and Degradation Model |
| R12-RES-7111 | P0 | invariant | **Adverse side effect: rollback, reduce authority, failure episode** — An intervention causing an adverse side effect triggers plan-specific stop/rollback/hedge/mitigation, reduces the relevant action-policy authority, creates a failure episode and requires re-evaluation before reuse. |  | unit: Observed side effect narrows authority and blocks reuse. | v11.6 p46 §28 Failure and Degradation Model |
| R12-RES-7112 | P0 | invariant | **Fabric broker loss: ISR failover, epoch retry, offset resume** — On regional broker loss partition leadership moves to an in-sync replica in another zone, producers retry by epoch, consumers resume from committed offsets, and reflex execution does not wait for failover. |  | game-day: Killing the leader promotes a replica with no committed loss. | v11.6 p46 §28 Failure and Degradation Model |
| R12-RES-7113 | P0 | invariant | **Fabric quorum lost: reflex continues, local queue bounded, degrade first** — On fabric quorum loss local reflex nodes continue from cached signed policy and local journals, new control changes stop, durable publication queues locally within bounded disk budgets, and policy-defined degradation happens before journal exhaustion. |  | integration: Spool pressure narrows sizing before it halts new exposure. | v11.6 p46 §28 Failure and Degradation Model |
| R12-RES-7114 | P1 | invariant | **Mirror down: local streams writable, lag visible, replay on return** — When the cross-region mirror is down local streams stay writable, mirror lag is visible and bounded, no synchronous cross-region quorum is attempted, and global consumers mark remote knowledge stale and replay from regional/GCS archive on return. | `MULTI_REGION` | game-day: Mirror lag gauge rises while local produce continues. | v11.6 p46 §28 Failure and Degradation Model |

## FINOPS

Cost governance, capacity, scaling.

| ID | P | Kind | Requirement | Flags | Verification | Source |
|---|---|---|---|---|---|---|
| R12-FINOPS-8040 | P1 | constraint | **Hot Reflex capacity reserved** — Hot Reflex capacity must be reserved with reservations/commitments. |  | plan: Each execution IGM consumes a specific reservation sized to its target; removing reservation_affinity must fail. | GCP v2.1 p4 §3 Global Region and Failure-Domain Strategy; GCP v2.1 p6 §6 Regional Reflex Cell |
| R12-FINOPS-8041 | P2 | constraint | **Warm, data and AI components autoscale** — Warm, data and AI components must autoscale where scaling time is compatible with workload behaviour. |  | plan: Plan shows autoscaling bounds for Cloud Run services and data stores. | GCP v2.1 p4 §3 Global Region and Failure-Domain Strategy |
| R12-FINOPS-9186 | P2 | constraint | **BigQuery separate research, backtest and BI reservations** — BigQuery uses separate research, backtest and BI reservations/workload management so no one job monopolises compute. | `MANAGED_SERVICE` `COST` | plan: Three reservations and assignments exist. | GCP v2.1 p19 §18 Scaling and Performance Model |
| R12-FINOPS-9188 | P3 | process | **AI training queue/quota-aware, Spot where safe, reserved capacity if justified** — AI training uses queue- and quota-aware scheduling, Spot where interruption is safe, and fixed reserved capacity for critical retraining only if justified. | `MANAGED_SERVICE` `COST` | plan: Job specs carry Spot/queue settings. | GCP v2.1 p19 §18 Scaling and Performance Model |
| R12-FINOPS-9189 | P3 | constraint | **Autonomous-dev workers scale independently with concurrency quotas** — Autonomous-dev ephemeral workers and build pools scale independently, with concurrency quotas protecting source APIs and budgets. | `COST` | plan: Worker pools have max concurrency. | GCP v2.1 p19 §18 Scaling and Performance Model |
| R12-FINOPS-10028 | P1 | constraint | **Reservations guarantee production hot capacity** — Production hot capacity (Reflex, Fabric) is backed by specific Compute Engine reservations sized to target; commitments are used to control cost. | `COST` | inspection: Every machine group consumes a specific reservation sized to its target; an unreserved group fails. | GCP v2.1 p20 §19 Complete GCP Service Catalog for This Platform; GCP v2.1 p28 §23 FinOps and Capacity Governance |
| R12-FINOPS-10134 | P1 | constraint | **Every resource labelled by plane, region, environment, owner and workload** — Every project/resource is labelled with plane, region, environment, owner and workload. | `COST` | inspection: A scan finds all five labels on every labelable resource. | GCP v2.1 p26 §23 FinOps and Capacity Governance |
| R12-FINOPS-10135 | P1 | capability | **Billing is exported to BigQuery** — Billing data is exported to BigQuery. | `COST` `MANAGED_SERVICE` | plan: A billing export dataset/config exists. | GCP v2.1 p26 §23 FinOps and Capacity Governance |
| R12-FINOPS-10136 | P2 | metric | **Cost reported per strategy, model, data source and region** — Cost is reported per strategy, model, data source and region where attribution is possible. | `COST` | unit: A cost ledger entry carries strategy/model/source/region keys. | GCP v2.1 p26 §23 FinOps and Capacity Governance |
| R12-FINOPS-10137 | P1 | constraint | **Hot path is overprovisioned; commitments only after stable sizing** — Reflex/Fabric capacity is overprovisioned for latency and failure headroom and is never reactively autoscaled; committed-use/reservations are optimised only after benchmark sizing is stable. | `COST` | inspection: No autoscaler on execution-node groups. | GCP v2.1 p26 §23 FinOps and Capacity Governance |
| R12-FINOPS-10138 | P1 | constraint | **Retention is tiered and derived duplicates are bounded** — Tick/journal retention is tiered: hot replay in Fabric/Bigtable, immutable segments in GCS, aggregates in BigQuery; derived duplicates are not retained forever. | `COST` | inspection: Every derived store carries a bounded retention/lifecycle rule. | GCP v2.1 p26 §23 FinOps and Capacity Governance |
| R12-FINOPS-10139 | P2 | constraint | **AI work has budgets, quotas and benefit-per-dollar benchmarks** — Agents and training families have budget/concurrency quotas, use Spot where safe, and model benefit is benchmarked per dollar and latency. | `COST` | unit: An agent exceeding its budget is stopped; cost per benefit is recorded. | GCP v2.1 p26 §23 FinOps and Capacity Governance |
| R12-FINOPS-10140 | P1 | constraint | **Autonomous agents have daily/monthly model, build and tool budgets** — Autonomous engineering agents get explicit daily/monthly model/build/tool budgets and can be throttled independently of production runtime. | `COST` `NON_RUST` | unit: A spent daily budget refuses further calls and is audited. | GCP v2.1 p26 §23 FinOps and Capacity Governance |
| R12-FINOPS-10141 | P2 | capability | **Cost anomalies raise Fabric/incident tasks** — Budgets plus Monitoring/BigQuery anomaly jobs create Fabric/incident tasks. | `COST` | integration: A billing anomaly fixture produces one journaled task. | GCP v2.1 p27 §23 FinOps and Capacity Governance |
| R12-FINOPS-10142 | P0 | invariant | **Cost actions never auto-delete production state** — No cost or budget action automatically deletes production state. | `COST` | inspection: Budgets carry no programmatic action; adding a Pub/Sub action fails. | GCP v2.1 p27 §23 FinOps and Capacity Governance |
